# AEGIS PMA — CIO Brief · Friday 2026-10-23

**Run state:** committee closed · 38 names on the floor (36 nominated + 2 held) · **9 ADVANCE · 17 HOLD-FOR-CONDITIONS · 12 PASS** · ranking purity PASS · crowding audit clean (gap +3%).

### Declared today (read first)

1. Seats ran as general-purpose agents reading their card and vote packet by path → Same method every seat; nothing inlined by hand
2. **The fundamentals voice is consulted only, no vote** (PM ruling 22 Oct) — 10 voters → His fundamentals read appears per name as information; it changed no verdict
3. Fundamentals now print in the vote packet (flat keys added) → Seats could see P/E, growth, FCF yield for the first time in the packet itself
4. VALEN block added to the vote packet as context (levels omitted under R1) → Seats saw stance, no-buy flags and U&R; no VALEN price levels reached a vote
5. Tiger now returns stop triggers → MP stop 36.66 and V stop 401.15 read correctly from the broker
6. Data gap: MP's injected row carried entry 53.00 (cost) in place of the last close 38.56 → Seats were told to read the live price; handover item
7. Isolation breach, self-declared: one seat glanced at another seat's round-1 form after deciding → Its picks were already set and the other seat filed zero nominations; its held review is flagged as possibly anchored
8. One seat's ballot carries bracket-mention flags on 11 names (worst-case gap uses the fallback stop) → Accepted as information; not a reason for any vote (R1)
9. No intraday tape for brackets; no dynCap on the committee side → All 25 brackets are **levels only, unsized**
10. Engine bracket invalid on 34 of 38 names; 6 names have 4 of 6 lenses not served (WRB, WCN, KDP, CHD, SHO, CCEP) → Exits below are the analysts' own levels, not the engine's
11. Repeat-watch (124 KB), PM-lens full table and the analyst-by-analyst conditions are pasted verbatim in Appendices B–D → Kept out of §0–§9C so the body reads on a phone

## §0A — EXECUTIVE CONSENSUS: WHY THESE ADVANCE

**The book in three lines.**
1. Seven of nine ADVANCE names sit **exactly at a 52-week high**; the median is ~3 ATR above its 20-day, and five of nine are defensives (3 staples, 2 healthcare) — this is a "leaders at highs" book bought into a Valen DOWNTREND tape.
2. Agreement was strongest on **ROKU** (near-unanimous) and **LTH** (broad, in the pullback lane); thinnest on **CHD** and the **V add** (narrow), and **MTCH** carried over a real objection.
3. The single condition that kills the group thesis: **SPY losing the 760 put wall** — in a negative-gamma tape that turns a quiet tape into an amplified sell-off, and defensives at highs are the first thing sold to raise cash.

**The uncomfortable scorecard (15–22 Oct, graded only, no verdict changed).** ADVANCE calls **beat SPY** (+1.48% mean, **+1.76 vs SPY, 31 of 39 beat it**) but **lagged the equal-weight AQE list by −1.41 points**. PASS names (−0.10 vs list) and near-misses (−0.06 vs list) did **better** than the ADVANCEs against the list. In plain terms: the committee picks beat the index but have not beaten simply owning the list. The only bright spot is the small full-horizon sample (8 ADVANCEs, +0.19 vs list).

**the fundamentals reviewer contradicts three ADVANCEs — stated, not acted on:** **KDP** (P/E 34.8 on 8.2/3.6/5.4% sales growth), **CHD** (P/E 34.9, sales growth slowing 1.6/4.1/9.2%) and **ROKU** (P/E 66.7 on 15.2% growth, PEG ~4.4). For these three, the upside rests on the trend, not on earnings.

### ROKU — ADVANCE · conviction 3
A fresh break to a new 52-week high on 2.8× normal volume, with the 50-, 100- and 200-day averages in order (155.20 > 147.24 > 126.32) and price only 3.1% above its 20-day — the room's cleanest breakout, not a chase. The case against was that a one-day volume burst in a Valen DOWNTREND is exactly where late money enters, and the 20-day (153.82) still sits under the 50-day; it lost because the trend structure, not the volume, carries the case. What must stay true: the break holds — **a daily close under 155.20 ends it**.

### MTCH — ADVANCE · conviction 3
A leader 1.3% under its 52-week high with momentum 82 and accelerating, averages in order (41.66 > 39.50 > 36.21), beating SPY by 12.97 points over 20 days. The losing argument was real: it is already 10.2% above its 20-day with only one of six lenses strong, and its force reading dropped from 10 to 7 on the last bar — a profit-taking bar, not an entry. It carried only because the buy is the new high itself on volume, never the prior close.

### LTH — ADVANCE · conviction 3
The strongest combined read in the room: 5 of 6 lenses strong, a fresh break of structure, and price sitting 0.45% above its 20-day after dipping under its 9-day average and closing back above (Valen U&R MET). The objection was that it is 8.2% under its high with overhead supply and is a pullback buy, which pure breakout methods refuse; it lost because the whole room decides the pullback lane and most of it read a held test. What must stay true: **no daily close under 40.43**.

### AMGN — ADVANCE · conviction 3
Healthcare leader at a new 52-week high with all four averages stacked (420.09 > 416.83 > 391.86 > 371.12) and four lenses strong; ELV at a high alongside it confirms the group. The case against: volume only 1.1× and flow 44.7 — the high is not being accumulated, and momentum is decelerating. It lost on trend and group strength, but the buy waits for **an hourly close above 450.98**, not today's price.

### KDP — ADVANCE · conviction 3
Staples leader at a new high on 2.4× volume, momentum 90 and accelerating, with CHD and CCEP making highs beside it. Against: a defensive stock moving on more than double volume is an event, and nobody can name the event; it is 10.1% above its 20-day and the fundamentals reviewer reads a fully priced slow grower. It carried narrowly on price action alone — the trigger is a further new high, not the prior close.

### CHD — ADVANCE · conviction 3
Third staples name at a high, momentum 94 and accelerating, with the 20-day still above the 50-day (99.43 > 98.56). The losing argument was the strongest of the day: relative-strength rank 69.3 is under the 70 floor, it is 12.2% past any buy point, Valen puts it on the no-buy list at **4.9 ATR extended**, and the fundamentals reviewer contradicts. It advanced narrowly; read it as **right name, wrong price** — the clean entry is back toward the 20-day at 99.43.

### CCEP — ADVANCE · conviction 3
Staples new high that keeps gaining ground on days the market falls (down-day RS +1.59), averages in order (105.50 > 104.28 > 99.95), 4.5% from its pivot. Against: 1.25× volume fails the breakout-volume test and 4 of 6 lenses were not served, so the mechanical read is an absence, not a confirmation. Carried over that objection; buy only **above 114.55**.

### ELV — ADVANCE · conviction 3
Healthcare partner to AMGN at a new high, momentum 90, averages stacked (411.74 > 407.21 > 401.68 > 370.77), and a tight inside day at the high — a clean break point. Against: 1.08× volume fails the volume test, with coil and institutional-money lenses both warning. Carried over that objection; the trigger is the **break of yesterday's high (449.95) on volume**.

### V — ADVANCE on the ADD · conviction 3 (held)
The held position is a leader at a new high, momentum 94, averages in order, and in profit (414.34 vs 413.26 cost) — the case is to add only on a further new high. The case against was close to winning: it is barely in profit (+0.26%), 10.1% above its 20-day, on Valen's no-buy list at 4.6 ATR, and MA in the same room is the same payments bet twice. **Narrow — a conditional add, not a buy-now.**

No name in the room carries a runner or pre-move detection tag; every momentum case is analyst judgement, not a mechanical detector.

**Stance: NEUTRAL** — up from RISK_OFF five days ago and RISK_OFF a month ago. SPY is below its short-term averages on both the daily and weekly charts; the index is in a downtrend.

> **VIV System doctrine (quoted, not an Aegis rule):** *"Mixed signals — half size, best setups only, take profit sooner."*

## §9C — THE DAY'S PLAN (SGT, Friday night)

1. **Before 21:30 — decide MP.** The committee case is exit; the broker stop at 36.66 is the floor if you hold. Decide before the open, not after a gap.
2. **21:30 open — no market entries.** Every buy here is an hourly close; ROKU and MTCH are already above their triggers, so watch that they hold VWAP rather than chase the open.
3. **21:45 — flash PMI (FMP labels it ISM).** A weak print backs the growth-scare read and the defensive names (KDP, CCEP, AMGN, ELV); a strong one with copper still falling is noise.
4. **22:30 — first hourly close.** Check ROKU > 158.85, MTCH > 44.94, LTH > 42.58, CCEP > 114.55, KDP > 35.40, AMGN > 450.98, ELV > 449.95 — each with volume and beating SPY.
5. **Mid-session plan-changers:** SPY loses **760** (put wall) → stand down on new entries; SPY reclaims **777.85** (gamma flip) → the amplified-swing warning lifts.
6. **Before 04:00 close:** any position opened today that is set to close under its exit level gets your decision before the bell; V's broker stop at 401.15 stays as is. FOMC is Wednesday 2026-10-28.
